LegendreK6.M2Pilot.completeBudget_le : LegendreK6.M2Pilot.completeBudget ≤ 234943 / 250000
'LegendreK6.M2Pilot.completeBudget_le' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
LegendreK6.M2Pilot.pilot_short_interval_from_inputs (HQ : LegendreK6.QRHSevenEighths) (HC : LegendreK6.CSv2)
  (HR : LegendreK6.RosserZeroCount) (HF : LegendreK6.IntegratedExplicitFormula) (x : ℝ) (hx : 0 < x)
  (hlo : 715 / 4 ≤ Real.log x) (hhi : Real.log x ≤ 179) : LegendreK6.ShortInterval6 x
'LegendreK6.M2Pilot.pilot_short_interval_from_inputs' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
def LegendreK6.QRHSevenEighths : Prop :=
∀ (ρ : LegendreK6.NontrivialZero), (↑ρ).re ≤ 7 / 8
def LegendreK6.CSv2 : Prop :=
∀ (i : Fin 12) (σ T : ℝ),
  (16 + ↑↑i) / 32 ≤ σ →
    σ ≤ (17 + ↑↑i) / 32 →
      3000000000000 ≤ T →
        ↑(LegendreK6.densityZeroCount σ T) ≤
          LegendreK6.csB1 i * T ^ LegendreK6.csExponent σ * Real.log T ^ LegendreK6.csLogExponent σ +
              LegendreK6.csB2 i * Real.log T ^ 2 +
            LegendreK6.csB3 i * Real.log T
def LegendreK6.RosserZeroCount : Prop :=
∀ (T : ℝ),
  2 ≤ T →
    |↑(LegendreK6.rosserCount T) - (T / (2 * Real.pi) * Real.log (T / (2 * Real.pi)) - T / (2 * Real.pi) + 7 / 8)| ≤
      137 / 1000 * Real.log T + 443 / 1000 * Real.log (Real.log T) + 397 / 250
def LegendreK6.IntegratedExplicitFormula : Prop :=
∀ (t : ℝ),
  1 < t → Filter.Tendsto (LegendreK6.integratedZeroSum t) Filter.atTop (nhds (LegendreK6.integratedFormulaValue t))
def LegendreK6.M2Pilot.completeBudget : ℝ :=
LegendreK6.M2Pilot.mainBudget + LegendreK6.M2Pilot.auxiliaryBudget +
    LegendreK6.middlePrimePowerBudget LegendreK6.M2Pilot.left LegendreK6.M2Pilot.right +
  LegendreK6.cellTrivialBudget LegendreK6.M2Pilot.left
def LegendreK6.integratedFormulaValue : ℝ → ℂ :=
fun t =>
  ↑t ^ 2 / 2 - deriv riemannZeta 0 / riemannZeta 0 * ↑t + deriv riemannZeta (-1) / riemannZeta (-1) -
      LegendreK6.trivialZeroPrimitiveSum t -
    ↑(LegendreK6.integratedPsi t)
